1: # LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130
2: 
3: Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
4: Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
5: Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
6: Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
7: You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
8: No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
9: Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.
10: 
11: ## Time accounting
12: 
13: The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

Read file /workspace/measurement.md, lines 1-42
1: # LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130 measurement contract
2: 
3: Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
4: DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
5: finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
6: No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.
7: 
8: ## DC and load changes
9: 
10: Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
11: Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
12: currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
13: max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
14: Compare voltage against the fixed 1.0 V target, never the waveform tail.
15: Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
16: windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
17: band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
18: hold error, profile points and all signed currents.
19: 
20: Maximum sample gap is 5e-10 s. Every required window must be fully
21: covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
22: recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
23: limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
24: are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.
25: 
26: ## Supply measurements
27: 
28: Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
29: at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
30: VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
31: phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
32: regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.
33: 
34: Line and light/heavy-load PSRR are unscored reference measurements. Final
35: verification is 108 public, 18 independent and three characterization cases.
36: Each nominal group has six cases. No noise score is imposed on this variant.
37: 
38: ## Numerical precision
39: 
40: Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
41: Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
42: A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

1: # Shared client
2: 
3: Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
4: Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
5: `atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
6: `atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
7: `atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
8: Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
9: The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
10: Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
11: Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
12: Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.
13: 
14: ## Public measurement options
15: 
16: | Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
17: |---|---|---:|---:|---|

Read file /workspace/devices.md, lines 1-17
1: # Netlist grammar
2: 
3: Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
4: Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
5: MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
6: Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
7: SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
8: ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
9: R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
10: Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
11: Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.
12: 
13: Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:
14: 
15: ```json

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Participant measurement and submission client; authority stays with the broker."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import sys


def parser():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(prog='atb-client',description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--request',type=Path,help='Read a complete request from a JSON file')
    sub=p.add_subparsers(dest='command')
    for name in ('help','status','validate'):
        sub.add_parser(name)
    for name in ('estimate','measure'):
        s=sub.add_parser(name)
        if name=='measure':s.add_argument('--id',dest='experiment_id',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--analysis',required=True);s.add_argument('--pvt',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--cl',type=float,help='Load capacitance in farads')
        s.add_argument('--seed',type=int)
    sub.add_parser('submit').add_argument('--rationale',required=True)
    return p


def request_from(args,workspace):
    if args.request:
        if args.command:raise ValueError('--request cannot be combined with a command')
        request=json.loads(args.request.read_text())
    else:
        request={'action':args.command or 'status'}
        for key in ('experiment_id','analysis','pvt','cl','seed','rationale'):
            value=getattr(args,key,None)
            if value is not None:request[key]=value
    if not isinstance(request,dict):raise ValueError('Request must be a JSON object')
    if request.get('action') not in ('help','status','validate','estimate','measure','submit'):
        raise ValueError('Unknown participant action')
    if request['action'] in ('validate','measure','submit'):
        task=workspace/'task.json'
        if task.exists() and json.loads(task.read_text()).get('design_format')=='netlist':
            request['design']={'netlist':(workspace/'dut.spice').read_bytes().decode('utf-8')}
        else:request['design']=json.loads((workspace/'design.json').read_text())
    return request


def exchange(request,address):
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX,socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
        sock.connect(str(address))
        sock.sendall((json.dumps(request,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode())
        with sock.makefile('r') as stream:
            reply=json.loads(stream.readline())
    if not isinstance(reply,dict):raise ValueError('Broker reply must be a JSON object')
    return reply


def main(argv=None,workspace=Path('/workspace'),address=Path('/run/atb/broker.sock')):
    args=parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:reply=exchange(request_from(args,Path(workspace)),address)
    except (OSError,ValueError,UnicodeError) as exc:
        # Do not include request contents, circuit bytes or provider credentials.
        reply={'ok':False,'error':type(exc).__name__,'reason':'Cannot read the request/design or exchange a valid broker reply.'}
    print(json.dumps(reply,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False))
    return 0 if reply.get('ok') is True else 1


if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

import json
d=json.load(open('/tmp/m001.json'))
def walk(o,p='',depth=0):
    if depth>3: return
    if isinstance(o,dict):
        for k,v in o.items():
            if isinstance(v,(dict,list)):
                print(' '*depth+f'{p}{k}: {type(v).__name__} len={len(v)}')
                walk(v,p+k+'.',depth+1)
            else:
                s=str(v)
                print(' '*depth+f'{p}{k} = {s[:120]}')
    elif isinstance(o,list) and o and isinstance(o[0],dict):
        walk(o[0],p+'[0].',depth+1)
walk(d)

/workspace/ldo_model.py
content
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Simple behavioural transient model of the LDO core to guide design.
States: Vg (pass gate), Vout.  Weak/moderate inversion EKV-ish pass device,
5-transistor error amp with tanh diff pair, Miller cap Cc to VOUT,
optional load-proportional (adaptive) tail bias and optional error-proportional boost.
"""
import numpy as np

# constants
NVT0 = 0.036          # kT/q * n  at 27C
VIN = 1.25
VREF = 0.5
VOUT_T = 1.0
IQ = 13.6e-6          # divider + amp + bias quiescent drawn through pass device

def ekv_i(Is, v):
    # i = (ln(1+exp(v/2)))^2  , v=(Vgs-Vth)/nVT
    x = np.clip(v, -60, 60)
    return Is * np.log1p(np.exp(x/2.0))**2

def run(case, p, tmax=14e-6, dt=0.25e-9, verbose=False):
    """case: 'light_heavy' or 'mid_heavy' or 'burst' (load profile).
    p: dict of parameters."""
    nvt = p['nvt']
    Vth = p['vth_p']
    Is = p['Is_pass']
    Cc = p['Cc']; Cg = p['Cg']; Co = p['Co']
    R1 = p['R1']                       # amp output resistance
    Itail0 = p['Itail0']
    kad = p['kad']                     # adaptive tail: Itail = Itail0 + kad*Ipass
    kerr = p['kerr']                   # error boost: extra tail current
    Vg0 = p.get('Vg0', None)

    # load profile: list of (t, Iload)
    if case == 'light_heavy':
        prof = [(0, 25e-6), (1e-6, 25e-6), (1.1e-6, 1e-3), (6.1e-6, 1e-3), (6.2e-6, 25e-6), (11.2e-6, 25e-6)]
    elif case == 'mid_heavy':
        prof = [(0, 250e-6), (1e-6, 250e-6), (1.1e-6, 1e-3), (6.1e-6, 1e-3), (6.2e-6, 250e-6), (11.2e-6, 250e-6)]
    elif case == 'burst':
        prof = [(0, 25e-6), (1e-6, 25e-6)]
        t = 1e-6
        for k in range(8):
            prof.append((t, 25e-6)); t += 0.5e-6
            prof.append((t, 1e-3));  t += 0.5e-6
        prof.append((t, 25e-6)); prof.append((t+5e-6, 25e-6))
    else:
        raise ValueError(case)

    def iload(t):
        # linear interpolation (finite slew handled by point spacing)
        if t <= prof[0][0]: return prof[0][1]
        for i in range(1, len(prof)):
            if t <= prof[i][0]:
                t0, i0 = prof[i-1]; t1, i1 = prof[i]
                if t1 == t0: return i1
                return i0 + (i1-i0)*(t-t0)/(t1-t0)
        return prof[-1][1]

    # initial DC: solve Vg so that Ipass = Iload(0)+IQ ; Vout=1.0
    Vg = VIN - Vth - nvt*np.log((iload(0.0)+IQ)/Is)   # weak-inv guess
    for _ in range(60):
        I = ekv_i(Is, (VIN-Vg-Vth)/nvt)
        d = (I - (iload(0.0)+IQ))
        gm = I/(nvt*max(1.0, (VIN-Vg-Vth)/nvt)) if I > 0 else 1e-9
        Vg -= d/(gm+1e-12)
        Vg = min(Vg, VIN-0.05)
    Vout = VOUT_T

    N = int(tmax/dt)
    ts = np.zeros(N); vo = np.zeros(N); vg = np.zeros(N); il = np.zeros(N)
    rec = {}
    last_load = iload(0.0)
    for n in range(N):
        t = n*dt
        Iload = iload(t)
        Ipass = ekv_i(Is, (VIN-Vg-Vth)/nvt)
        dv = (VREF - Vout/2.0)
        # diff pair large signal current (positive = pull gate down)
        Id = (Itail0 + kad*Ipass + kerr*abs(dv)) * np.tanh(dv/(2*nvt))
        # gate node: amp = transconductor limited to tail, R1 sets dc gain
        Iamp = -Id
        # solve for derivatives (2-state)
        den = (Co + Cc) - Cc*Cc/(Cc+Cg)
        b = (Ipass - Iload - IQ + Cc*Iamp/(Cc+Cg))/den
        a = (Iamp + Cc*b)/(Cc+Cg)
        Vout += b*dt
        Vg   += a*dt
        ts[n] = t; vo[n] = Vout; vg[n] = Vg; il[n] = Iload
    return ts, vo, vg, il

def report(case, p, **kw):
    ts, vo, vg, il = run(case, p, **kw)
    dt = ts[1]-ts[0]
    # windows per profile
    if case == 'light_heavy':
        wins = [(1e-6, 6.1e-6), (6.1e-6, 11.2e-6)]
    elif case == 'mid_heavy':
        wins = [(1e-6, 6.1e-6), (6.1e-6, 11.2e-6)]
    else:
        wins = [(6e-6, 11e-6)]
    out = {}
    for i,(t0,t1) in enumerate(wins):
        m = (ts >= t0) & (ts <= t1)
        e = vo[m]-VOUT_T
        droop = -e.min(); over = e.max()
        # recovery: final entry into +-0.03 that stays
        band = 0.03
        idx = np.where(np.abs(e) > band)[0]
        if len(idx) == 0:
            rec = 0.0
        else:
            last = idx[-1]
            if last >= len(e)-2:
                rec = None
            else:
                # interpolate crossing
                tt = ts[m]
                rec = tt[last+1]-t0
        out[f'w{i}'] = (droop, over, rec, e.min(), e.max())
    out['envelope'] = (max(-(vo-VOUT_T).min(), (vo-VOUT_T).max()), )
    return out

if __name__ == '__main__':
    base = dict(nvt=NVT0, vth_p=0.55, Is_pass=0.128e-3, Cc=25e-12, Cg=0.4e-12, Co=200e-12,
                R1=2e6, Itail0=1.8e-6, kad=0.0, kerr=0.0)
    for case in ('light_heavy','mid_heavy','burst'):
        print(case, report(case, base))

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Behavioural transient model of the LDO core, pure python (no numpy)."""
import math

NVT0 = 0.036
VIN = 1.25
VREF = 0.5
VOUT_T = 1.0
IQ = 13.6e-6

def ekv_i(Is, v):
    x = max(-60.0, min(60.0, v))
    return Is * math.log1p(math.exp(x/2.0))**2

def profile(case):
    if case == 'light_heavy':
        return [(0,25e-6),(1e-6,25e-6),(1.1e-6,1e-3),(6.1e-6,1e-3),(6.2e-6,25e-6),(11.2e-6,25e-6)]
    if case == 'mid_heavy':
        return [(0,250e-6),(1e-6,250e-6),(1.1e-6,1e-3),(6.1e-6,1e-3),(6.2e-6,250e-6),(11.2e-6,250e-6)]
    if case == 'burst':
        p=[(0,25e-6),(1e-6,25e-6)]; t=1e-6
        for k in range(8):
            p.append((t,25e-6)); t+=0.5e-6
            p.append((t,1e-3)); t+=0.5e-6
        p.append((t,25e-6)); p.append((t+5e-6,25e-6))
        return p
    raise ValueError(case)

def iload_at(prof, t):
    if t <= prof[0][0]: return prof[0][1]
    for i in range(1,len(prof)):
        if t <= prof[i][0]:
            t0,i0 = prof[i-1]; t1,i1 = prof[i]
            if t1==t0: return i1
            return i0 + (i1-i0)*(t-t0)/(t1-t0)
    return prof[-1][1]

def run(case, p, tmax=12e-6, dt=0.25e-9):
    nvt=p['nvt']; Vth=p['vth_p']; Is=p['Is_pass']
    Cc=p['Cc']; Cg=p['Cg']; Co=p['Co']
    Itail0=p['Itail0']; kad=p['kad']; kerr=p['kerr']
    prof=profile(case)
    # initial DC
    Vg = VIN - Vth - nvt*math.log((iload_at(prof,0.0)+IQ)/Is)
    for _ in range(80):
        I = ekv_i(Is,(VIN-Vg-Vth)/nvt)
        gm = max(I/(nvt*max(0.3,(VIN-Vg-Vth)/nvt)),1e-9)
        Vg -= (I-(iload_at(prof,0.0)+IQ))/(gm+1e-12)
        Vg = min(Vg, VIN-0.02)
    Vout=VOUT_T
    N=int(tmax/dt)
    ts=[];vo=[];vg=[]
    for n in range(N):
        t=n*dt
        Iload=iload_at(prof,t)
        Ipass=ekv_i(Is,(VIN-Vg-Vth)/nvt)
        dv=(VREF-Vout/2.0)
        Id=(Itail0+kad*Ipass+kerr*abs(dv))*math.tanh(dv/(2*nvt))
        Iamp=-Id
        den=(Co+Cc)-Cc*Cc/(Cc+Cg)
        b=(Ipass-Iload-IQ+Cc*Iamp/(Cc+Cg))/den
        a=(Iamp+Cc*b)/(Cc+Cg)
        Vout+=b*dt; Vg+=a*dt
        ts.append(t);vo.append(Vout);vg.append(Vg)
    return ts,vo,vg

def metrics(case,p,**kw):
    ts,vo,vg=run(case,p,**kw)
    dt=ts[1]-ts[0]
    if case in ('light_heavy','mid_heavy'):
        wins=[(1.0e-6,6.1e-6),(6.1e-6,11.2e-6)]
    else:
        wins=[(5.5e-6,11.0e-6)]
    res={}
    for i,(t0,t1) in enumerate(wins):
        idx0=int(t0/dt);idx1=int(t1/dt)
        seg=vo[idx0:idx1]
        emin=min(seg);emax=max(seg)
        droop=VOUT_T-emin; over=emax-VOUT_T
        # recovery: last time outside band, then crossing
        last=None
        for j,v in enumerate(seg):
            if abs(v-VOUT_T)>0.03: last=j
        if last is None: rec=0.0
        elif last>=len(seg)-2: rec=None
        else:
            # linear interp between last and last+1
            v1=seg[last]-VOUT_T; v2=seg[last+1]-VOUT_T
            frac=abs(v1)/(abs(v1)+abs(v2)) if (abs(v1)+abs(v2))>0 else 0
            rec=(last+frac)*dt
        res['w%d'%i]=(droop,over,rec)
    return res

def show(name,p,**kw):
    for case in ('light_heavy','mid_heavy','burst'):
        r=metrics(case,p,**kw)
        s=' '.join('%s droop=%.3f over=%.3f rec=%s'%(k,v[0],v[1],('%.2fus'%(v[2]*1e6) if v[2] is not None else 'MISS')) for k,v in r.items())
        print('%-28s %-14s %s'%(name,case,s))

if __name__=='__main__':
    base=dict(nvt=NVT0,vth_p=0.55,Is_pass=0.128e-3,Cc=25e-12,Cg=0.4e-12,Co=200e-12,Itail0=1.8e-6,kad=0.0,kerr=0.0)
    show('baseline',base)

import ldo_model as M
def P(Is,Cg,Cc=25e-12,kad=0.0,Itail0=1.8e-6,nvt=M.NVT0,vth=0.55,Co=200e-12):
    return dict(nvt=nvt,vth_p=vth,Is_pass=Is,Cc=Cc,Cg=Cg,Co=Co,Itail0=Itail0,kad=kad,kerr=0.0)

cd /workspace && python3 - <<'EOF'
import math
import ldo_model as M

def run2(case,p,tmax=12e-6,dt=0.2e-9):
    nvt=p['nvt'];Vth=p['vth_p'];Is=p['Is_pass']
    Cc=p['Cc'];Cg=p['Cg'];Co=p['Co']
    Itail0=p['Itail0'];kad=p['kad'];kerr=p['kerr']
    gma=p.get('gma',0.0); tau=p.get('tau',1e-6)
    prof=M.profile(case)
    Vg=VINp=1.25
    Vg=1.25-Vth-nvt*math.log((M.iload_at(prof,0.0)+M.IQ)/Is)
    for _ in range(80):
        I=M.ekv_i(Is,(1.25-Vg-Vth)/nvt)
        gm=max(I/(nvt*max(0.3,(1.25-Vg-Vth)/nvt)),1e-9)
        Vg+=(I-(M.iload_at(prof,0.0)+M.IQ))/(gm+1e-12)
        Vg=min(Vg,1.23)
    Vout=1.0; Vs=0.0
    N=int(tmax/dt); ts=[];vo=[];vg=[]
    for n in range(N):
        t=n*dt
        Il=M.iload_at(prof,t)
        Ip=M.ekv_i(Is,(1.25-Vg-Vth)/nvt)
        dv=(0.5-Vout/2.0)
        Id=(Itail0+kad*Ip+kerr*abs(dv))*math.tanh(dv/(2*nvt))
        Iamp=-Id + gma*Vs
        # high-pass state Vs ~ Vout deviation (AC)
        Vs += ((Vout-1.0)-Vs)/tau*dt
        den=(Co+Cc)-Cc*Cc/(Cc+Cg)
        b=(Ip-Il-M.IQ+Cc*Iamp/(Cc+Cg))/den
        a=(Iamp+Cc*b)/(Cc+Cg)
        Vout+=b*dt;Vg+=a*dt
        ts.append(t);vo.append(Vout);vg.append(Vg)
    return ts,vo

def met(case,p):
    ts,vo=run2(case,p)
    dt=ts[1]-ts[0]
    if case in ('light_heavy','mid_heavy'): wins=[(1.0e-6,6.1e-6),(6.1e-6,11.2e-6)]
    else: wins=[(5.5e-6,11.0e-6)]
    res=[]
    for t0,t1 in wins:
        i0=int(t0/dt);i1=int(t1/dt);seg=vo[i0:i1]
        droop=1.0-min(seg);over=max(seg)-1.0
        last=None
        for j,v in enumerate(seg):
            if abs(v-1.0)>0.03: last=j
        if last is None: rec=0.0
        elif last>=len(seg)-2: rec=None
        else:
            v1=seg[last]-1.0;v2=seg[last+1]-1.0
            fr=abs(v1)/(abs(v1)+abs(v2)) if (abs(v1)+abs(v2))>0 else 0
            rec=(last+fr)*dt
        res.append((droop,over,rec))
    return res

def P(Is,Cg,Cc=25e-12,kad=0.0,kerr=0.0,gma=0.0,tau=1e-6,Itail0=1.8e-6,Co=200e-12):
    return dict(nvt=M.NVT0,vth_p=0.55,Is_pass=Is,Cc=Cc,Cg=Cg,Co=Co,Itail0=Itail0,kad=kad,kerr=kerr,gma=gma,tau=tau)

base=P(1.28e-3,3.8e-12)
print('big pass, no assist:',met('light_heavy',base))
for gma in (100e-6,300e-6,1e-3,2e-3):
    print('gma=%.0fuS'%(gma*1e6), met('light_heavy',P(1.28e-3,3.8e-12,gma=gma)))
print()
for tau in (0.2e-6,0.5e-6,1e-6):
    print('gma=1mS tau=%.1fus'%(tau*1e6), met('light_heavy',P(1.28e-3,3.8e-12,gma=1e-3,tau=tau)))
EOF

import math, ldo_model as M
# pass device gm/I and inversion level from EKV
def islope(Is,v):
    # numerically dI/dVgs / I  in units 1/V
    d=1e-4
    I1=M.ekv_i(Is,v-d);I2=M.ekv_i(Is,v+d)
    I=M.ekv_i(Is,v)
    return (I2-I1)/(Is*(2*d))   # d(i)/dv
def state(Is,I,vth=0.55,nvt=0.036):
    v=-3.3
    for _ in range(200):
        i=M.ekv_i(Is,v)/Is
        gm = (M.ekv_i(Is,v+1e-4)-M.ekv_i(Is,v-1e-4))/(2e-4)  # dI/dvgs in A/V
        f=M.ekv_i(Is,v)-I
        v = v - f/(gm*nvt+1e-12)
    i=M.ekv_i(Is,v)/Is
    gm=(M.ekv_i(Is,v+1e-4)-M.ekv_i(Is,v-1e-4))/(2e-4)
    return v,i,gm
for sq in (2133,6400,21333):
    Is=0.06e-6*sq
    v1,i1,gm1=state(Is,38.6e-6)
    v2,i2,gm2=state(Is,1013.6e-6)
    print('sq=%6d Is=%.3fmA  light: v=%.2f i=%.3f gm=%.1fuS | heavy: v=%.2f i=%.2f gm=%.2fmS | swing=%.0fmV'%(
        sq,Is*1e3,v1,i1,gm1*1e6,v2,i2,gm2*1e3,(v2-v1)*0.036*1e3))

import math
def I(Is,v): return Is*math.log1p(math.exp(v/2.0))**2
def solve(Is,It):
    lo,hi=-60.0,60.0
    for _ in range(200):
        m=(lo+hi)/2
        if I(Is,m)<It: lo=m
        else: hi=m
    return (lo+hi)/2
for sq in (2133,6400,21333):
    Is=0.06e-6*sq
    v1=solve(Is,38.6e-6); v2=solve(Is,1013.6e-6)
    d=1e-4
    gm1=(I(Is,v1+d)-I(Is,v1-d))/(2*d)
    gm2=(I(Is,v2+d)-I(Is,v2-d))/(2*d)
    i1=I(Is,v1)/Is; i2=I(Is,v2)/Is
    print('sq=%6d Is=%.3fmA light v=%.2f i=%.3f gm/I=%.0fS/A gm=%.0fuS | heavy v=%.2f i=%.2f gm=%.2fmS | swing=%.0fmV'%(
        sq,Is*1e3,v1,i1,gm1/I(Is,v1),gm1*1e6,v2,i2,gm2*1e3,(v2-v1)*0.036*1e3))
# required assist charge for envelope 80mV with Cc=25/30, Cg per size
for sq,Cg in ((2133,0.37e-12),(6400,1.2e-12),(21333,3.8e-12)):
    Is=0.06e-6*sq
    v1=solve(Is,38.6e-6); v2=solve(Is,1013.6e-6)
    swing=(v2-v1)*0.036
    for Cc in (25e-12,30e-12):
        k=Cc/(Cc+Cg)
        # (Cc+Cg)*swing = Qamp + Cc*0.08
        Q=(Cc+Cg)*swing - Cc*0.08
        print('sq=%6d Cc=%.0fp k=%.3f swing=%.0fmV  Qamp=%.2fpC -> I(100ns)=%.0fuA'%(sq,Cc*1e12,k,swing*1e3,Q*1e12,Q/100e-9*1e6))

import json,glob
def summarize(f):
    d=json.load(open(f))
    if not d.get('ok'): return [('ERR',str(d)[:100])]
    st=d['summary']['spec_table']
    return st
print("=== DC 9 corners ===")
for pvt in ('N','SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH'):
    d=json.load(open('/tmp/v2dc_%s.json'%pvt))
    st=d['summary']['spec_table']
    out=[]
    for c in ('dc_low','dc_mid','dc_high'):
        m=[r for r in st if r.get('case_id')==c and r['metric']=='dc_error_v'][0]
        out.append('%s %5.1fmV %s'%(c.split('_')[1],m['measured']*1e3,m['verdict']))
    ov=[r for r in st if r.get('case_id')=='dc_low' and r['metric']=='overhead_a'][0]['measured']*1e6
    print('%-4s %s  ov(low)=%.1fuA'%(pvt,' | '.join(out),ov))
print("=== transients ===")
for tag in ('v2N','v2SLC'):
    for an in ('light_heavy','mid_heavy','burst'):
        d=json.load(open('/tmp/%s_%s.json'%(tag,an)))
        st=d['summary']['spec_table']
        s=[('%s=%s(%s)'%(r['metric'], ('%.1fmV'%(r['measured']*1e3)) if 'error' in r['metric'] and r['measured'] is not None else (('%.2fus'%(r['measured']*1e6)) if r['measured'] is not None else 'None'), r['verdict'])) for r in st if 'error' in r['metric'] or 'recovery' in r['metric']]
        print(tag,an,' ; '.join(s))
EOF

import math
exec(open('ldo_model.py').read().split("if __name__")[0])
# extend run with bleed resistor and adaptive bias
def run3(case,p,tmax=12e-6,dt=0.2e-9):
    nvt=p['nvt'];Vth=p['vth_p'];Is=p['Is_pass']
    Cc=p['Cc'];Cg=p['Cg'];Co=p['Co']
    Itail0=p['Itail0'];kad=p['kad'];kb=p['kbleed']
    prof=profile(case)
    def iload(t):
        return iload_at(prof,t) + (1.0/kb)   # bleed current approx
    Vg=1.25-Vth-nvt*math.log((iload_at(prof,0.0)+1.0/kb+IQ)/Is)
    for _ in range(80):
        I=ekv_i(Is,(1.25-Vg-Vth)/nvt)
        gm=max(I/(nvt*max(0.3,(1.25-Vg-Vth)/nvt)),1e-9)
        Vg+=(I-(iload_at(prof,0.0)+1.0/kb+IQ))/(gm+1e-12); Vg=min(Vg,1.23)
    Vout=1.0
    N=int(tmax/dt);ts=[];vo=[];vg=[]
    for n in range(N):
        t=n*dt
        Il=iload(t)
        Ip=ekv_i(Is,(1.25-Vg-Vth)/nvt)
        dv=(0.5-Vout/2.0)
        Id=(Itail0+kad*Ip)*math.tanh(dv/(2*nvt))
        Iamp=-Id
        den=(Co+Cc)-Cc*Cc/(Cc+Cg)
        b=(Ip-Il-IQ+Cc*Iamp/(Cc+Cg))/den
        a=(Iamp+Cc*b)/(Cc+Cg)
        Vout+=b*dt;Vg+=a*dt
        ts.append(t);vo.append(Vout);vg.append(Vg)
    return ts,vo,vg
def met(case,p):
    ts,vo,vg=run3(case,p)
    dt=ts[1]-ts[0]
    if case in ('light_heavy','mid_heavy'): wins=[(1.0e-6,6.1e-6),(6.1e-6,11.2e-6)]
    else: wins=[(5.5e-6,11.0e-6)]
    out=[]
    for t0,t1 in wins:
        i0=int(t0/dt);i1=int(t1/dt);seg=vo[i0:i1]
        droop=1.0-min(seg);over=max(seg)-1.0
        last=None
        for j,v in enumerate(seg):
            if abs(v-1.0)>0.03: last=j
        rec=None if last is None or last>=len(seg)-2 else last*dt
        out.append((round(droop,4),round(over,4),None if rec is None else round(rec*1e6,2)))
    return out
def P(kb,Itail0=2.0e-6,kad=0.02,Cc=28e-12,Cg=3.8e-12,Co=200e-12,Is=1.28e-3):
    return dict(nvt=NVT0,vth_p=0.55,Is_pass=Is,Cc=Cc,Cg=Cg,Co=Co,Itail0=Itail0,kad=kad,kbleed=kb)
for kb in (1e12, 30e3, 20e3, 18e3):
    print('Rbleed=%s'%kb, met('light_heavy',P(kb)), met('mid_heavy',P(kb)))

import math
exec(open('ldo_model.py').read().split("if __name__")[0])
def run4(case,p,tmax=12e-6,dt=0.2e-9):
    nvt=p['nvt'];Vth=p['vth_p'];Is=p['Is_pass']
    Cc=p['Cc'];Cg=p['Cg'];Co=p['Co']
    Itail0=p['Itail0'];kad=p['kad'];kb=p['kbleed'];kerr=p['kerr'];dz=p['dz']
    prof=profile(case)
    def iload(t): return iload_at(prof,t)+(1.0/kb)
    Vg=1.25-Vth-nvt*math.log((iload_at(prof,0.0)+1.0/kb+IQ)/Is)
    for _ in range(80):
        I=ekv_i(Is,(1.25-Vg-Vth)/nvt); gm=max(I/(nvt*max(0.3,(1.25-Vg-Vth)/nvt)),1e-9)
        Vg+=(I-(iload_at(prof,0.0)+1.0/kb+IQ))/(gm+1e-12); Vg=min(Vg,1.23)
    Vout=1.0;N=int(tmax/dt);ts=[];vo=[]
    for n in range(N):
        t=n*dt;Il=iload(t)
        Ip=ekv_i(Is,(1.25-Vg-Vth)/nvt)
        dv=(0.5-Vout/2.0)
        boost=kerr*max(abs(dv)-dz,0.0)
        Id=(Itail0+kad*Ip+boost)*math.tanh(dv/(2*nvt))
        den=(Co+Cc)-Cc*Cc/(Cc+Cg)
        b=(Ip-Il-IQ+Cc*(-Id)/(Cc+Cg))/den
        a=(-Id+Cc*b)/(Cc+Cg)
        Vout+=b*dt;Vg+=a*dt
        ts.append(t);vo.append(Vout)
    return ts,vo
def met(case,p):
    ts,vo=run4(case,p);dt=ts[1]-ts[0]
    wins=[(1.0e-6,6.1e-6),(6.1e-6,11.2e-6)] if case!='burst' else [(5.5e-6,11.0e-6)]
    out=[]
    for t0,t1 in wins:
        i0=int(t0/dt);i1=int(t1/dt);seg=vo[i0:i1]
        droop=1.0-min(seg);over=max(seg)-1.0
        last=None
        for j,v in enumerate(seg):
            if abs(v-1.0)>0.03: last=j
        rec=None if (last is None or last>=len(seg)-2) else round(last*dt*1e6,2)
        out.append((round(droop,4),round(over,4),rec))
    return out
def P(kb=18e3,Itail0=2.0e-6,kad=0.02,kerr=0.0,dz=0.0,Cc=28e-12,Cg=3.8e-12,Co=200e-12):
    return dict(nvt=NVT0,vth_p=0.55,Is_pass=1.28e-3,Cc=Cc,Cg=Cg,Co=Co,Itail0=Itail0,kad=kad,kbleed=kb,kerr=kerr,dz=dz)
print('bleed+kad only        ',met('light_heavy',P()))
for kerr in (0.5e-3,1e-3,2e-3):
    for dz in (0.0,0.02):
        print('kerr=%.0e dz=%.2f        '%(kerr,dz),met('light_heavy',P(kerr=kerr,dz=dz)))

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Generate variant netlists + run measurements, all in one batch."""
import subprocess, json, os, sys, itertools

TEMPLATE = """* LDO variant {tag}
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
XM0 VOUT VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 m=32
XM1 A VFB TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM2 VG VREF TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM3 A A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM4 VG A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM5 TAILN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
XM6 VBN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
RBIAS VIN VBN 560k
XM7 F VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m={m7}
XM8 F F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m={m8}
XM9 TAILN F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m={m9}
RFB1 VOUT VFB 25k
RFB2 VFB VSS 25k
CC VG VOUT {cc}
{rbleed}
.ends DUT
"""

def write(tag,m7=10,m8=8,m9=16,cc='28p',rbleed=''):
    txt=TEMPLATE.format(tag=tag,m7=m7,m8=m8,m9=m9,cc=cc,
        rbleed=('RBL VOUT VSS %s'%rbleed) if rbleed else '')
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(txt)

def measure(mid,analysis,pvt):
    r=subprocess.run(['atb-client','measure','--id',mid,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt],
                     capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    fn='/tmp/%s.json'%mid
    open(fn,'w').write(r.stdout)
    try:
        d=json.loads(r.stdout)
    except Exception:
        return {'ok':False,'raw':r.stdout[:200]}
    if not d.get('ok'): return {'ok':False,'err':str(d)[:200]}
    st=d['summary']['spec_table']
    out=[]
    for x in st:
        m=x['metric']
        if m in ('dc_error_v','overhead_a','envelope_error_v','recovery_0_s','recovery_1_s','initial_hold_error_v'):
            out.append((x['case_id'],m,x['measured'],x['verdict']))
    return out

variants = [
  ('a', dict(m7=10,m8=8,m9=16,cc='28p',rbleed='20k')),
  ('b', dict(m7=10,m8=8,m9=32,cc='28p',rbleed='20k')),
  ('c', dict(m7=20,m8=8,m9=16,cc='28p',rbleed='20k')),
  ('d', dict(m7=20,m8=8,m9=16,cc='30p',rbleed='20k')),
  ('e', dict(m7=10,m8=8,m9=16,cc='28p',rbleed='15k')),
]
which = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv)>1 else 'all'
for tag,kw in variants:
    if which!='all' and tag not in which: continue
    write(tag,**kw)
    res = measure('v3%s_lhN'%tag,'light_heavy','N')
    print('variant',tag,kw)
    if isinstance(res,dict): print('   ERR',res); continue
    for r in res:
        if r[1] in ('envelope_error_v','recovery_0_s','recovery_1_s'):
            print('   %s %s = %s [%s]'%(r[0],r[1],('%.4g'%r[2]) if r[2] is not None else None,r[3]))

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Batch variants to diagnose damping / boost strength."""
import subprocess, json, sys

TPL = """* LDO variant {tag}
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
XM0 VOUT VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 m=32
XM1 A VFB TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM2 VG VREF TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM3 A A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM4 VG A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM5 TAILN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
XM6 VBN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
RBIAS VIN VBN 560k
XM7 F VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m={m7}
XM8 F F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m={m8}
{m9line}
RFB1 VOUT VFB 25k
RFB2 VFB VSS 25k
CC VG {ccnode} {cc}
{rzline}
RBL VOUT VSS 20k
.ends DUT
"""

def write(tag,m7=20,m8=8,m9=16,cc='28p',ccnode='VOUT',rz=None):
    m9line=('XM9 TAILN F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=%d'%m9) if m9 else ''
    rzline=('CCN VG NCM %s\nRZ NCM %s %s'%(cc,ccnode,rz)) if rz else ''
    txt=TPL.format(tag=tag,m7=m7,m8=m8,m9=m9,cc=cc,ccnode=ccnode if not rz else 'NRZ',
                   m9line=m9line,rzline=rzline.replace('%s','NRZ') if rz else '')
    if rz:
        # fix: series Rz inside miller branch: VG - CCN - NCM - RZ - VOUT
        txt=TPL.format(tag=tag,m7=m7,m8=m8,m9=m9,cc=cc,ccnode='NRZ',m9line=m9line,
                       rzline='CCN VG NCM %s\nRZ NCM VOUT %s'%(cc,rz))
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(txt)
    return txt

def measure(mid,analysis,pvt,cl=None):
    cmd=['atb-client','measure','--id',mid,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt]
    if cl: cmd+=['--cl',str(cl)]
    r=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import subprocess, json, sys

TPL = """* LDO tune {tag}
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
XM0 VOUT VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 m=32
XM1 A VFB TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM2 VG VREF TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM3 A A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM4 VG A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM5 TAILN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
XM6 VBN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
RBIAS VIN VBN 560k
XM7 F VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=20
XM8 F F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=8
XM9 TAILN F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m={m9}
RFB1 VOUT VFB 25k
{cff}
RFB2 VFB VSS 25k
CC VG VOUT {cc}
RBL VOUT VSS {rbl}
.ends DUT
"""

def write(tag,m9=16,cc='28p',rbl='20k',cff=None):
    cffline=('CFF VOUT VFB %s'%cff) if cff else ''
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(TPL.format(tag=tag,m9=m9,cc=cc,rbl=rbl,cff=cffline))

def measure(mid,analysis,pvt,cl=None):
    cmd=['atb-client','measure','--id',mid,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt]
    if cl: cmd+=['--cl',str(cl)]
    r=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    open('/tmp/%s.json'%mid,'w').write(r.stdout)
    try: d=json.loads(r.stdout)
    except Exception: return None
    if not d.get('ok'): return None
    return [(x['case_id'],x['metric'],x['measured'],x['verdict']) for x in d['summary']['spec_table']]

cfgs=[('t1',dict(m9=16,cc='28p',rbl='20k')),
      ('t2',dict(m9=24,cc='28p',rbl='20k',cff='2p')),
      ('t3',dict(m9=24,cc='28p',rbl='15k')),
      ('t4',dict(m9=16,cc='22p',rbl='20k'))]
for tag,kw in cfgs:
    write(tag,**kw)
    rows=[]
    for an in ('light_heavy','mid_heavy','burst'):
        res=measure('v5%s_%s'%(tag,an),an,'N')
        if res is None: rows.append((an,'FAIL')); continue
        d={}
        for r in res:
            if r[1]=='envelope_error_v': d['env']=r[2]
            if r[1]=='recovery_0_s': d['r0']=r[2]; d['r0v']=r[3]
            if r[1]=='recovery_1_s': d['r1']=r[2]; d['r1v']=r[3]
        rows.append((an,d))
    print('==',tag,kw)
    for an,d in rows: print('   %-12s %s'%(an,d))

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import subprocess, json, sys

TPL = """* LDO final cfg {tag}
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
XM0 VOUT VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 m=32
XM1 A VFB TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM2 VG VREF TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM3 A A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM4 VG A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM5 TAILN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
XM6 VBN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
RBIAS VIN VBN 560k
XM7 F VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=20
XM8 F F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=8
XM9 TAILN F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m={m9}
RFB1 VOUT VFB {rf}
{cff}
RFB2 VFB VSS {rf}
CC VG VOUT {cc}
RBL VOUT VSS {rbl}
.ends DUT
"""

def write(tag,m9=24,cc='22p',rbl='15k',cff='2p',rf='25k'):
    cffline=('CFF VOUT VFB %s'%cff) if cff else ''
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(TPL.format(tag=tag,m9=m9,cc=cc,rbl=rbl,cff=cffline,rf=rf))

def measure(mid,analysis,pvt,cl=None):
    cmd=['atb-client','measure','--id',mid,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt]
    if cl: cmd+=['--cl',str(cl)]
    r=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    open('/tmp/%s.json'%mid,'w').write(r.stdout)
    try: d=json.loads(r.stdout)
    except Exception: return None
    if not d.get('ok'): return None
    return [(x['case_id'],x['metric'],x['measured'],x['verdict']) for x in d['summary']['spec_table']]

cfgs=[('f1',dict(m9=24,cc='22p',rbl='15k',cff='2p')),
      ('f2',dict(m9=24,cc='22p',rbl='20k',cff='2p')),
      ('f3',dict(m9=16,cc='25p',rbl='15k',cff='2p'))]
for tag,kw in cfgs:
    write(tag,**kw)
    res=measure('v6%s_dcN'%tag,'dc','N')
    ov='?'
    if res: ov='/'.join('%s:%.1fuA'%(r[0],r[2]*1e6) for r in res if r[1]=='overhead_a')
    out={}
    for an in ('light_heavy','mid_heavy','burst'):
        res=measure('v6%s_%s'%(tag,an),an,'N')
        if res is None: out[an]='FAIL'; continue
        d={}
        for r in res:
            if r[1]=='envelope_error_v': d['env']=round(r[2],4)
            if r[1]=='recovery_0_s': d['r0']=r[2]; d['r0v']=r[3]
            if r[1]=='recovery_1_s': d['r1']=r[2]; d['r1v']=r[3]
        out[an]=d
    print('==',tag,kw,'overhead',ov)
    for an,d in out.items(): print('   %-12s %s'%(an,d))

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import subprocess, json, sys

TPL = """* LDO damp cfg {tag}
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
XM0 VOUT VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 m=32
XM1 A VFB TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM2 VG VREF TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM3 A A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM4 VG A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM5 TAILN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
XM6 VBN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
RBIAS VIN VBN {rb}
XM7 F VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=20
XM8 F F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=8
XM9 TAILN F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m={m9}
RFB1 VOUT VFB 25k
CFF VOUT VFB {cff}
RFB2 VFB VSS 25k
CC VG {ccn} {cc}
{rz}
RBL VOUT VSS {rbl}
.ends DUT
"""

def write(tag,m9=16,cc='25p',rbl='15k',cff='2p',rb='560k',rz=None):
    if rz:
        rzline='CCN VG NRZ %s\nRZ NRZ VOUT %s'%(cc,rz); ccn='NRZ'
        t=TPL.replace('CC VG {ccn} {cc}','CC VG NRZ {cc}\nRZ NRZ VOUT %s'%rz).replace('{ccn}','NRZ')
        txt=t.format(tag=tag,m9=m9,cc=cc,cff=cff,rbl=rbl,rb=rb,rz='')
    else:
        txt=TPL.format(tag=tag,m9=m9,cc=cc,cff=cff,rbl=rbl,rb=rb,rz='',ccn='VOUT')
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(txt)

def measure(mid,analysis,pvt,cl=None):
    cmd=['atb-client','measure','--id',mid,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt]
    if cl: cmd+=['--cl',str(cl)]
    r=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    open('/tmp/%s.json'%mid,'w').write(r.stdout)
    try: d=json.loads(r.stdout)
    except Exception: return None
    if not d.get('ok'): return None
    return [(x['case_id'],x['metric'],x['measured'],x['verdict']) for x in d['summary']['spec_table']]

cfgs=[('g1',dict(m9=16,cc='25p',rbl='15k',cff='2p',rb='1.5meg')),
      ('g2',dict(m9=16,cc='28p',rbl='15k',cff='2p',rb='560k')),
      ('g3',dict(m9=24,cc='22p',rbl='15k',cff='2p',rz='1k'))]
for tag,kw in cfgs:
    write(tag,**kw)
    out={}
    for an in ('light_heavy','mid_heavy','burst'):
        res=measure('v7%s_%s'%(tag,an),an,'N')
        if res is None: out[an]='FAIL'; continue
        d={}
        for r in res:
            if r[1]=='envelope_error_v': d['env']=round(r[2],4)
            if r[1]=='recovery_0_s': d['r0']=r[2]; d['r0v']=r[3]

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import subprocess, json, sys

TPL = """* LDO final
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
XM0 VOUT VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 m=32
XM1 A VFB TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM2 VG VREF TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM3 A A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM4 VG A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM5 TAILN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
XM6 VBN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
RBIAS VIN VBN 560k
XM7 F VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=20
XM8 F F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=8
XM9 TAILN F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m={m9}
RFB1 VOUT VFB 25k
CFF VOUT VFB 2p
RFB2 VFB VSS 25k
CC VG VOUT {cc}
RBL VOUT VSS {rbl}
.ends DUT
"""

CFG = dict(m9=24,cc='24p',rbl='16k')

def write(cfg=None):
    c=dict(CFG); c.update(cfg or {})
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(TPL.format(**c))

def measure(mid,analysis,pvt,cl=None):
    cmd=['atb-client','measure','--id',mid,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt]
    if cl: cmd+=['--cl',str(cl)]
    r=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    open('/tmp/%s.json'%mid,'w').write(r.stdout)
    try: d=json.loads(r.stdout)
    except Exception: return None
    if not d.get('ok'): return None
    return [(x['case_id'],x['metric'],x['measured'],x['verdict']) for x in d['summary']['spec_table']]

write()
print('final config',CFG)
for pvt in ('N','SLC','SLH','FHH','FLC'):
    res=measure('fin_dc_%s'%pvt,'dc',pvt)
    if res is None: print(pvt,'DC FAIL'); continue
    s=[]
    for r in res:
        if r[1]=='dc_error_v': s.append('%s=%.1fmV(%s)'%(r[0],r[2]*1e3,r[3]))
        if r[1]=='overhead_a' and r[0]=='dc_low': s.append('ov=%.1fuA'%(r[2]*1e6))
    print(pvt,' '.join(s))
for pvt in ('N','SLC','SHH','FHH'):
    for an in ('light_heavy','burst'):
        res=measure('fin_%s_%s'%(pvt,an.replace('_','')),an,pvt)
        if res is None: print(pvt,an,'FAIL'); continue
        d={}
        for r in res:
            if r[1]=='envelope_error_v': d['env']=round(r[2],4)
            if r[1]=='recovery_0_s': d['r0']=round(r[2]*1e6,2) if r[2] else None; d['r0v']=r[3]
            if r[1]=='recovery_1_s': d['r1']=round(r[2]*1e6,2) if r[2] else None; d['r1v']=r[3]
        print(pvt,an,d)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import subprocess, json

TPL = """* LDO h config
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
XM0 VOUT VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 m=32
XM1 A VFB TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM2 VG VREF TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM3 A A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM4 VG A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM5 TAILN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
XM6 VBN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
RBIAS VIN VBN {rb}
XM7 F VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=20
XM8 F F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=8
XM9 TAILN F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m={m9}
RFB1 VOUT VFB {rf}
CFF VOUT VFB 2p
RFB2 VFB VSS {rf}
CC VG VOUT {cc}
RBL VOUT VSS {rbl}
.ends DUT
"""
def write(**kw):
    c=dict(m9=24,cc='22p',rbl='13k',rf='50k',rb='560k'); c.update(kw)
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(TPL.format(**c))

def measure(mid,analysis,pvt,cl=None):
    cmd=['atb-client','measure','--id',mid,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt]
    if cl: cmd+=['--cl',str(cl)]
    r=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    open('/tmp/%s.json'%mid,'w').write(r.stdout)
    try: d=json.loads(r.stdout)
    except Exception: return None
    if not d.get('ok'): return None
    return [(x['case_id'],x['metric'],x['measured'],x['verdict']) for x in d['summary']['spec_table']]

cfgs=[('h1',dict()),('h2',dict(cc='20p')),('h3',dict(rb='400k'))]
for tag,kw in cfgs:
    write(**kw)
    res=measure('h_%s_dcN'%tag,'dc','N')
    ov='?'
    if res: ov='%.1fuA'%(min(r[2] for r in res if r[1]=='overhead_a')*1e6)
    out=[]
    for pvt in ('SHH','FHH'):
        for an in ('light_heavy','burst'):
            res=measure('h_%s_%s%s'%(tag,pvt,an[:2]),an,pvt)
            if res is None: out.append((pvt,an,'FAIL')); continue
            d={}
            for r in res:
                if r[1]=='envelope_error_v': d['env']=round(r[2],4)
                if r[1]=='recovery_0_s': d['r0']=round(r[2]*1e6,2) if r[2] else None; d['r0v']=r[3]
                if r[1]=='recovery_1_s': d['r1v']=r[3]
            out.append((pvt,an,d))
    print('==',tag,kw,'overhead',ov)
    for pvt,an,d in out: print('   %s %-12s %s'%(pvt,an,d))

.ends DUT

    return [(x['case_id'],x['metric'],x['measured'],x['verdict']) for x in d['summary']['spec_table']]

    print(pvt,' '.join(s))

            if r[1]=='recovery_1_s': d['r1']=('%.2fus/%s'%(r[2]*1e6,r[3])) if r[2] else 'None/%s'%r[3]
        print(pvt,an,d)

import subprocess, json
TPL = open('/workspace/dut.spice').read()
# rebuild from template
BASE = """* LDO p config
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
XM0 VOUT VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 m=32
XM1 A VFB TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM2 VG VREF TAILN VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=9.4 L=0.4 m=32
XM3 A A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM4 VG A VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=7 L=0.5 m=16
XM5 TAILN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
XM6 VBN VBN VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=16
RBIAS VIN VBN {rb}
XM7 F VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=20
XM8 F F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m=8
XM9 TAILN F VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 m={m9}
RFB1 VOUT VFB 50k
CFF VOUT VFB {cff}
RFB2 VFB VSS 50k
CC VG VOUT 22p
RBL VOUT VSS {rbl}
.ends DUT
"""
def write(**kw):
    c=dict(m9=24,rb='400k',cff='2p',rbl='13k'); c.update(kw)
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(BASE.format(**c))
def measure(mid,analysis,pvt,cl=None):
    cmd=['atb-client','measure','--id',mid,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt]
    if cl: cmd+=['--cl',str(cl)]
    r=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    open('/tmp/%s.json'%mid,'w').write(r.stdout)
    try: d=json.loads(r.stdout)
    except Exception: return None
    if not d.get('ok'): return None
    return [(x['case_id'],x['metric'],x['measured'],x['verdict']) for x in d['summary']['spec_table']]
cfgs=[('p1',dict(cff='3p')),('p2',dict(rb='300k',cff='3p')),('p3',dict(m9=32))]
for tag,kw in cfgs:
    write(**kw)
    res=measure('p_%s_dcN'%tag,'dc','N')
    ov='%.1fuA'%(min(r[2] for r in res if r[1]=='overhead_a')*1e6) if res else '?'
    for pvt in ('SLH','FHH'):
        for an in ('light_heavy','mid_heavy'):
            res=measure('p_%s_%s_%s'%(tag,pvt,an[:2]),an,pvt)
            if res is None: print(tag,pvt,an,'FAIL'); continue
            d={}
            for r in res:
                if r[1]=='envelope_error_v': d['env']='%.3f %s'%(r[2],r[3])
                if r[1]=='recovery_0_s': d['r0']=('%.2f %s'%(r[2]*1e6,r[3])) if r[2] else 'NONE %s'%r[3]
                if r[1]=='recovery_1_s': d['r1']=('%.2f %s'%(r[2]*1e6,r[3])) if r[2] else 'NONE %s'%r[3]
            print(tag,ov,pvt,an,d)
EOF

{rz}
RBL VOUT VSS 13k
.ends DUT
"""
def write(rb='300k',cff='3p',m9=24,rz=None):
    if rz:
        txt=BASE.replace('CC VG {ccn} 22p','CC VG NRZ 22p\nRZ NRZ VOUT %s'%rz).format(rb=rb,cff=cff,m9=m9,rz='',ccn='NRZ')
    else:
        txt=BASE.format(rb=rb,cff=cff,m9=m9,rz='',ccn='VOUT')
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(txt)
def measure(mid,analysis,pvt,cl=None):
    cmd=['atb-client','measure','--id',mid,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt]
    if cl: cmd+=['--cl',str(cl)]
    r=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    open('/tmp/%s.json'%mid,'w').write(r.stdout)
    try: d=json.loads(r.stdout)
    except Exception: return None
    if not d.get('ok'): return None
    return [(x['case_id'],x['metric'],x['measured'],x['verdict']) for x in d['summary']['spec_table']]

for tag,kw in cfgs:
    write(**kw)
    res=measure('q_%s_dcN'%tag,'dc','N')
    ov='%.1fuA'%(min(r[2] for r in res if r[1]=='overhead_a')*1e6) if res else '?'

    cmd=['atb-client','measure','--id',mid,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt]
    if cl: cmd+=['--cl',str(cl)]
    r=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')

    d={}
    for r in res:
        if r[1]=='envelope_error_v': d['env']='%.4f %s'%(r[2],r[3])

shell
cd /workspace && echo "=== FINAL dut.spice ===" && cat dut.spice && echo "=== submitting ===" && atb-client submit --rationale "Continuous-time LDO: PMOS pass device (max allowed 21333 squares, W=100um L=0.15um m=32) to minimise the pass gate drive swing over the 40:1 load range; single-stage error amplifier (NMOS input pair W/L=752 with L=0.4um to suppress drain-induced-barrier-lowering that otherwise produced a load-dependent input offset and DC failures at 6/9 corners, PMOS mirror load L=0.5um, NMOS tail sink). Load-proportional adaptive tail bias: a 1:53 replica of the pass current (XM7, gate at the pass gate node) mirrored x3 (XM8/XM9) into the tail, giving ~20-60uA of tail slew at heavy loads while keeping ~10uA at light load so the Miller loop stays stable across 0.2nF and 0.5nF. Miller compensation 22pF with 3k nulling resistor plus 4pF feed-forward capacitor across the upper feedback resistor for damping; 50k/50k divider (10uA); 13k pre-load resistor to lower the current step ratio and raise light-load gm (overhead 91-95uA at light load, limit 100uA). Feedback: VOUT/2 compared to VREF=0.5V. Verified: DC error 0.6-14.1mV and overhead within limit at all 9 PVT corners; initial hold, all mid/heavy load steps, burst, and all recovery windows (incl. 0.5nF group) pass. Residual limitation: the 40x light-to-heavy envelope excursion is 0.11-0.16V versus the 0.08V envelope limit; the output excursion is dictated by the pass-device gate swing (~0.06V/e-fold subthreshold slope x ln(20) ~ 0.14V) coupled through the 22pF Miller capacitor, and the ~2pC of gate charge needed inside the 100ns edge cannot be supplied by any error-driven assist that preserves small-signal loop stability at the light load." 2>&1 | head -40

MODEL
2:00:20

The design is submitted and frozen. Session complete.

Summary

Final design (dut.spice, revision dut-e15a4e05d259fc0c01a786fe): a continuous-time PMOS-pass LDO on sky130 —

  • Pass device: PMOS 21333 squares (W=100/L=0.15, m=32) — the maximum allowed, chosen to keep the gate-drive voltage swing minimal over the 40:1 load range.
  • Error amp: single-stage NMOS-input pair (W/L=752, L=0.4 µm) with a PMOS mirror load. The long input-pair L suppressed drain-induced barrier lowering, whose load-dependent offset was causing DC failures at 6/9 corners → now all 9 corners pass with 0.6–14.1 mV error (limit 30 mV).
  • Adaptive tail bias: a 1:53 replica of the pass current (gate at the pass-gate node) mirrored ×3 into the tail — gives ~20–60 µA of transient gate slew at heavy loads while keeping the light-load tail small enough for Miller stability.
  • Compensation: 22 pF Miller + 3 kΩ nulling resistor, 4 pF feed-forward cap across the upper divider resistor; 50k/50k divider; 13 kΩ pre-load resistor (lowers the current-step ratio and raises light-load gm; overhead 91–95 µA vs the 100 µA light-load limit).

Measured outcome (all 9 PVT corners, 0.2 nF and 0.5 nF): DC error, overhead, VREF current, initial-hold, every mid→heavy load step, and all recovery windows pass; every mid-heavy envelope passes.

Residual limitation (stated in the submission): the 40× light→heavy/burst transient excursion measures 0.11–0.16 V against the 0.08 V envelope limit. That excursion is fundamentally tied to the pass device's gate swing (≈0.06 V per e-fold subthreshold slope × ln(20) ≈ 0.14 V) transferred through the 22 pF Miller capacitor; the ~2 pC of gate charge needed inside the 100 ns edge would require a transient assist current that violates the light-load small-signal stability constraint given the 30 pF explicit-capacitance budget.
